<div class="flex-column pt-5 pb-5">
<h2 class="h1 text-white">Can you trust SolarReviews?</h2>
<p class="text-secondary mb-5 text-uppercase font-weight-bold letter-spacing-wide">Behind the brand</p>
<p class="text-white">No solar company can pay to alter their reviews score, what reviews are displayed or where their company appears on SolarReviews.</p>
<p class="text-white">We do everything we can to ensure you can trust the reviews you read on SolarReviews. We have a reviews auditing team that try to ensure reviews published are genuine.</p>
<p class="text-white">We use an in house developed algorithm as well as manual spot checks to help with this. We have caught hundreds of solar companies trying to write false positive reviews and we have banned quite a few from our site.</p>
<p class="text-white">False negative reviews are also a problem that we work to eliminate. This is where competitors seek to unfairly damage the reputation of another company. We also use technology and manual checks to prevent publishing of false negative reviews. All reviews are vetted by a real person before being published.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div> <div class="bg-white bg-white-gradient-overlay-to-left">
<div class="container mb-5 mb-lg-0 pt-5 pt-lg-0">
<div class="row">
<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 overlay-content d-flex align-items-center">
<div class="flex-column pt-5 pb-5">
<h2 class="h1">Our Mission</h2>
<p class="text-secondary mb-5 text-uppercase font-weight-bold letter-spacing-wide">Who we are</p>
<p>SolarReviews is dedicated to ensuring you have access to unbiased information about solar and can make a decision about whether solar panels are right for you based on accurate information, not based on pressure from solar salespeople.</p>
<p>We believe that if we put quality information on the internet, and if that information is easily understood by consumers, that consumers are less likely to be misled by solar companies and will be more likely to be satisfied with their solar system.</p>
<p>We have already seen a number of instances where comments by our journalists or by consumers posting reviews on our site about unfair or misleading practises have stamped these practises out in the industry.  One such example is solar companies offering quotes that do not detail the brand and models of equipment being supplied. (This gives the solar company the opportunity to use cheaper brands to install jobs).<br/>
</p><p>It was once the case that most large solar companies did this. However, as a direct result of our exposure of this issue this practise is almost gone from the industry.
</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-12 col-lg-6">
<div class="position-relative h-100 vw-50 float-left d-none d-lg-block">
<div class="h-100">
<img alt="SolarReviews mission" class="h-100 vw-75" src="https://www.solarreviews.com/images/bg/homepage/our-mission.jpg"/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 overlay-content d-flex align-items-center">
<div class="flex-column pt-5 pb-5">
<h2 class="h1 text-white">How do we make money?</h2>
<p class="text-secondary mb-5 text-uppercase font-weight-bold letter-spacing-wide">How we work</p>
<p class="text-white">When viewing reviews of solar companies or specific solar equipment on our site  you may decide you want to request a quote.</p>
<p class="text-white">Approximately  2% of our site visitors make this decision and when they do we earn a small fixed fee from the solar company you request a quote from. This fee is the same regardless of which solar companies you request a quote from in your area.</p>
<p class="text-white">For the other 98% of our site visitors we are just a very high quality consumer education website providing content and calculators for free to evaluate the potential of solar panels for your home.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div> <div class="bg-white">
<div class="container mt-5 mb-5">
<div class="row pb-5 pb-md-0">
<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 overlay-content d-flex align-items-center">
<div class="flex-column pt-5 pb-5">
<h2 class="h1 mb-5">What are
                        <br/><a href="https://www.solarreviews.com/prescreened-solar-pros">Pre-Screened Solar Pros</a><br/> on SolarReviews?
                    </h2>
<p>As you browse our site you will notice some companies are designated as <a href="https://www.solarreviews.com/prescreened-solar-pros">Pre-Screened Solar Pros</a>.</p>
<p>These are a group of experienced and highly regarded solar companies that voluntarily provide extra information to us each year, such as insurance and contractors licensing information and have managed to maintain a required number of reviews each year at an above average reviews score. This badge does not indicate a specific financial relationship between SolarReviews and this company but is generally indicative of a high quality solar panel installation company.</p>
</div>

<script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            const searchForm = document.querySelector('#home_page_company_search_form');
            const companyNameInput = searchForm.querySelector('input[name="search_company_name"]');
            const searchButton = searchForm.querySelector('button[name="search"]');
            const companyTypesContainer = searchForm.querySelector('#company_types_container');
            const companyType = companyTypesContainer.querySelector('a:first-child');

            const baseUrl = 'https://www.solarreviews.com/';

            autocomplete({
                input: companyNameInput,
                fetch: function (term, update) {
                    getCompanies(term, companyType.getAttribute('data-company-type')).then(function (data) {
                        update(data);
                    });
                },
                onSelect: function (item) {
                    companyNameInput.value = item.value;
                },
                customize: function(input, inputRect, container, maxHeight) {
                    // Remove the default container width to show full company name in the drop down
                    container.style.width = '';
                    container.style.minWidth = inputRect.width + 'px';
                    container.style.maxWidth = (searchForm.offsetWidth * 0.90) + 'px';

                    container.querySelectorAll('div').forEach(function(element) { element.classList.add('py-1') });
                }
            });

            companyTypesContainer.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
                event.preventDefault();
                const targetElement = event.target;

                if (targetElement.tagName === 'A') {
                    companyType.innerText = targetElement.innerText;
                    companyType.setAttribute('data-company-type', targetElement.getAttribute('data-company-type'));
                }
            });

            searchButton.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
                event.preventDefault();

                searchForm.action = getAction();
                searchForm.method = getMethod();
                searchForm.submit();
            });

            function getAction() {
                const type = companyType.getAttribute('data-company-type');

                if (type === 'installer') {
                    return baseUrl + 'solar-companies/';
                }

                //change the name of the input field depending on the url we are submitting the form
                companyNameInput.setAttribute('name', 'search_' + type);

                return baseUrl + 'solar-' + type + '-reviews/';
            }

            function getMethod() {
                if (companyType.getAttribute('data-company-type') === 'installer') {
                    return 'POST';
                }

                return 'GET';
            }

            function getCompanies(term, type) {
                switch (type) {
                    case 'installer':
                        return getInstallers(term, type);
                    case 'panel':
                        return getManufacturers(term, 'panels');
                    case 'inverter':
                        return getManufacturers(term, 'inverters');
                    case 'battery':
                        return getManufacturers(term, 'batteries');
                }
            }

            function getInstallers(term, type) {
                return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
                    axios.get(baseUrl + 'remote/companies', {
                        params: {
                            term: term,
                            'company_type': type
                        }
                    }).then(function (response) {
                        resolve(response.data.map(function (value) {
                            return {label: value, value: value};
                        }).slice(0, 5));
                    }).catch(function (e) {
                        reject(e);
                    })
                });
            }

            function getManufacturers(term, brand) {
                return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
                    axios.get(baseUrl + 'brands/' + brand + '/search/' + term).then(function (response) {
                        resolve(response.data.map(function (value) {
                            return {label: value.companyname, value: value.companyname};
                        }).slice(0, 5));
                    }).catch(function (e) {
                        reject(e)
                    });
                });
            }
        });
    </script>
